- **1password-env-migrator**: Use to **get plaintext secrets off disk and into 1Password** — Phase D of the `1password` skill. Owns the **plaintext inventory** (`tools/op_guard.py scan-tree | scan-env | scan-mcp`, or the `op_scan_*` MCP tools — keys by name, never values), **1Password Environments** (create, import a `.env`, copy the Environment ID, AWS Secrets Manager sync), **locally mounted `.env` files** (named pipe, Mac/Linux, 25 mounts per device, watcher caveats), the **MCP config rewrite** (`"command": "op", "args": ["run", "--environment", ID, "--", …]`), removal of shell-profile exports and stray backups, the **… ([lurodrisilva/personal-skills](https://github.com/lurodrisilva/personal-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/1password-env-migrator.md))
